([EggProject/easter-skills-hack-to-hermes](https://github.com/EggProject/easter-skills-hack-to-hermes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Read-only pass over the codebase (or the current diff) for injection, broken auth and access control, secrets in code and unsafe input handling. Reports exploitable findings with a proof-of-concept path and fix; never edits. ([einargudnig/dotfiles](https://github.com/einargudnig/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Read-only security review of Hammertime code, focused on the agent-ingestion boundary (auth, validation, rate limiting, dedup) and anything handling untrusted input. Read-only — no edits, and Bash fenced by a guard hook to read-only inspection tooling. Emits findings as JSON only. Use after coder finishes a change touching services/ingest, auth, or any externally-reachable API. ([einot/Hammertime](https://github.com/einot/Hammertime/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Read-only security review of stratafs code, focused on the the NFS wire boundary (auth, validation, rate limiting, dedup) and anything handling untrusted input. Read-only — no edits, and Bash fenced by a guard hook to read-only inspection tooling. Emits findings as JSON only. Use after coder finishes a change touching the externally-reachable surface, auth, or any public API. ([einot/StrataFS](https://github.com/einot/StrataFS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Security auditor - auth/session flows, tenancy isolation, CSP/security headers, secrets hygiene, dependency risks, prompt-injection fences in LLM stacks. Use in audits and for any auth/tenancy/LLM-safety change. Via swarm-director only, never ad hoc. ([ekamphuis82/claude-code-swarm](https://github.com/ekamphuis82/claude-code-swarm/blob/HEAD/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Auditoria de segurança do CCMA: injeção, autenticação, autorização, IDOR, segredos, uploads, SSRF, criptografia, fail-open e dependências. Obrigatória no nível 3 da skill ccma (superfície sensível); vulnerabilidade crítica bloqueia a entrega. ([ElkysOfficial/.github](https://github.com/ElkysOfficial/.github/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **security-auditor**: Read-only OWASP Top 10 security auditor. Scans the codebase for vulnerabilities: broken access control, injection, cryptographic failures, SSRF, insecure config, hardcoded secrets, missing auth checks, and more. Reports findings with severity levels and line-specific remediation. Does NOT modify files. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: EPCI Phase 2 security audit. Checks OWASP Top 10, defense-in-depth, and sensitive configurations. Invoked if auth/security files detected. ([epci971/claude-epci](https://github.com/epci971/claude-epci/blob/HEAD/archive/4.8/agents/security-auditor.md) · 16 copies)
- **security-auditor**: Reviews code exclusively for security vulnerabilities - OWASP top 10, input validation, auth and authz, secrets exposure, crypto use, path traversal, error leakage, dependency risk. Records findings as `note` chunks for cross-session awareness. ([erkandogan/sodium](https://github.com/erkandogan/sodium/blob/HEAD/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Read-only security sweep of a change area or module — input validation, auth/authz, secrets, unsafe file/DB/API access. Use during review or repo-wide audits. ([ETGLap/claudeSTARTER](https://github.com/ETGLap/claudeSTARTER/blob/HEAD/.claude/templates/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Security engineer focused on vulnerability detection, threat modeling, and secure coding practices. Use for security-focused code review, threat analysis, or hardening recommendations. AEO release reviewer. Premium tier, always; never downgraded (Gate 8 checks the model it actually ran on). ([ezno12/aeo-orchestrator](https://github.com/ezno12/aeo-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: 檢查 SQL injection、XSS、密碼暴露、CSRF、認證漏洞等安全問題 ([f97214/2026-ai-adv-homework-course01](https://github.com/f97214/2026-ai-adv-homework-course01/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/security-auditor.md))
- **security-auditor**: Security audit against the OWASP Top 10:2025 (A01-A10). - **security-auditor**: Specialized security analysis agent that performs comprehensive vulnerability assessments, identifies security misconfigurations, analyzes code for security flaws, checks compliance with security standards (OWASP, SANS), and provides detailed remediation guidance.
